| `fancymenu.elements.video_mcef.loop` | Loop Video: %s |  |
| `fancymenu.elements.video_mcef.sound_channel` | Sound Channel: %s |  |
| `fancymenu.elements.video_mcef.volume` | Volume |  |
| `fancymenu.actions.video.set_video_element_volume` | Set Video Element Volume |  |
| `fancymenu.actions.video.set_video_element_volume.desc` | Sets the volume of a Video element.&lt;br&gt;The volume has to be a valid decimal between 0.0 (0%%) and 1.0 (100%%). |  |
| `fancymenu.actions.video.set_video_element_volume.value.desc` | Video Element Identifier + Volume [ Separated by : ] |  |
| `fancymenu.placeholders.video_element_volume` | Video Element Volume |  |
| `fancymenu.placeholders.video_element_volume.desc` | Returns the volume of a Video element.&lt;br&gt;The volume is a decimal between 0.0 (0%%) and 1.0 (100%%). |  |
| `fancymenu.placeholders.categories.video` | Video |  |
| `fancymenu.actions.video.toggle_paused_state` | Toggle Video Element Paused State |  |
| `fancymenu.actions.video.toggle_paused_state.desc` | Toggles the paused state of a Video element. |  |
| `fancymenu.actions.video.toggle_paused_state.value.desc` | Video Element Identifier |  |
| `fancymenu.placeholders.video_element_duration` | Video Element Duration |  |
| `fancymenu.placeholders.video_element_duration.desc` | Returns the total duration of a Video element in MM:SS format (e.g. '03:45').&lt;br&gt;Returns '00:00' if the element is not ready yet or not video is set.&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;Required values:&lt;br&gt;- element_identifier: The ID of the Video element to get the duration from |  |
| `fancymenu.placeholders.video_element_playtime` | Video Element Play Time |  |
| `fancymenu.placeholders.video_element_playtime.desc` | Returns either the current play time of a Video element&lt;br&gt;(MM:SS format) or the play progress percentage (0-100).&lt;br&gt;Returns '00:00' or '0' if the element is not ready yet or no video is set.&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;Required values:&lt;br&gt;- element_identifier: The ID of the Video element&lt;br&gt;- show_percentage: Set to 'true' to show percentage instead of time |  |
| `fancymenu.placeholders.video_paused_state` | Video Element Paused State |  |
| `fancymenu.placeholders.video_paused_state.desc` | Returns the paused state of a Video element.&lt;br&gt;Returns 'true' when the element is paused, otherwise returns 'false'. |  |
| `fancymenu.backgrounds.video_mcef` | Video [MCEF] |  |
| `fancymenu.backgrounds.video_mcef.desc` | Plays a video file as menu background.&lt;br&gt;§xThis background needs the MCEF mod to work! |  |
| `fancymenu.backgrounds.video_mcef.configure` | Configure Video Background |  |
| `fancymenu.backgrounds.video_mcef.configure.no_video` | §xYou need to choose a video first! |  |
| `fancymenu.actions.video.set_video_background_volume` | Set Video Background Volume |  |
| `fancymenu.actions.video.set_video_background_volume.desc` | Sets the volume of a Video menu background by its identifier.&lt;br&gt;The volume has to be a valid decimal between 0.0 (0%%) and 1.0 (100%%).&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;To get the identifier of a background, right-click the&lt;br&gt;editor background and click on 'Copy Background Identifier'. |  |
| `fancymenu.actions.video.set_video_background_volume.value.desc` | Video Background Identifier + Volume [ Separated by : ] |  |
| `fancymenu.actions.video.background.toggle_paused_state` | Toggle Video Background Paused State |  |
| `fancymenu.actions.video.background.toggle_paused_state.desc` | Toggles the paused state of a Video menu background by its identifier.&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;To get the identifier of a background, right-click the&lt;br&gt;editor background and click on 'Copy Background Identifier'. |  |
| `fancymenu.actions.video.background.toggle_paused_state.value.desc` | Video Background Identifier |  |
| `fancymenu.placeholders.video_background_duration` | Video Background Duration |  |
| `fancymenu.placeholders.video_background_duration.desc` | Returns the total duration of a Video menu background in MM:SS format (e.g. '03:45') by its identifier.&lt;br&gt;Returns '00:00' if the background is not ready yet or not video is set.&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;To get the identifier of a background, right-click the&lt;br&gt;editor background and click on 'Copy Background Identifier'. |  |
| `fancymenu.placeholders.video_background_paused_state` | Video Background Paused State |  |
| `fancymenu.placeholders.video_background_paused_state.desc` | Returns the paused state of a Video menu background by its identifier.&lt;br&gt;Returns 'true' when the element is paused, otherwise returns 'false'.&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;To get the identifier of a background, right-click the&lt;br&gt;editor background and click on 'Copy Background Identifier'. |  |
| `fancymenu.placeholders.video_background_playtime` | Video Background Play Time |  |
| `fancymenu.placeholders.video_background_playtime.desc` | Returns either the current play time of a Video menu background&lt;br&gt;(MM:SS format) or the play progress percentage (0-100).&lt;br&gt;Returns '00:00' or '0' if the element is not ready yet or no video is set.&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;Required values:&lt;br&gt;- background_identifier: The ID of the Video background&lt;br&gt;- show_percentage: Set to 'true' to show percentage instead of time&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;To get the identifier of a background, right-click the&lt;br&gt;editor background and click on 'Copy Background Identifier'. |  |
| `fancymenu.placeholders.video_background_volume` | Video Background Volume |  |
| `fancymenu.placeholders.video_background_volume.desc` | Returns the volume of a Video menu background by its identifier.&lt;br&gt;The volume is a decimal between 0.0 (0%%) and 1.0 (100%%).&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;To get the identifier of a background, right-click the&lt;br&gt;editor background and click on 'Copy Background Identifier'. |  |
| `fancymenu.editor.background_options.copy_background_identifier` | Copy Background Identifier |  |
| `fancymenu.editor.background_options.copy_background_identifier.desc` | This will copy the identifier of the&lt;br&gt;current menu background to the clipboard.&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;The background identifier is used by actions, placeholders&lt;br&gt;and similar stuff to communicate with the background instance. |  |
| `fancymenu.editor.buddy.enable` | Enable Buddy: %s |  |
| `fancymenu.editor.buddy.enable.desc` | If a cute little pet creature should&lt;br&gt;walk around at the bottom of the editor screen. |  |
| `fancymenu.elements.tooltip` | Tooltip |  |
| `fancymenu.elements.tooltip.desc` | Renders a tooltip at a specific position or the mouse position.&lt;br&gt;Has full Markdown support, similar to Text elements.&lt;br&gt;Control its visibility with loading requirements. |  |
| `fancymenu.elements.tooltip.content` | Tooltip Text Content |  |
| `fancymenu.elements.tooltip.background` | Tooltip Background |  |
| `fancymenu.elements.tooltip.background_texture` | Custom Background Texture |  |
| `fancymenu.elements.tooltip.background_texture.desc` | Set a custom background texture.&lt;br&gt;Uses nine-slicing to resize the texture for the current tooltip size. |  |
| `fancymenu.elements.tooltip.nine_slice` | Nine-Slicing Settings |  |
| `fancymenu.elements.tooltip.nine_slice.desc` | Here you can set settings related to the&lt;br&gt;nine-slicing logic of the custom tooltip background. |  |
| `fancymenu.elements.tooltip.nine_slice.border.top` | Top Border Size [Pixels] |  |
| `fancymenu.elements.tooltip.nine_slice.border.right` | Right Border Size [Pixels] |  |
| `fancymenu.elements.tooltip.nine_slice.border.bottom` | Bottom Border Size [Pixels] |  |
| `fancymenu.elements.tooltip.nine_slice.border.left` | Left Border Size [Pixels] |  |
| `fancymenu.elements.tooltip.mouse_following` | Follow Mouse: %s |  |
| `fancymenu.elements.tooltip.mouse_following.desc` | If the tooltip should follow the mouse when outside the editor. |  |
| `fancymenu.placeholders.text_char_count` | Text Character Count |  |
| `fancymenu.placeholders.text_char_count.desc` | Returns the character count of the given text. |  |
| `fancymenu.placeholders.text_width` | Text Width |  |
| `fancymenu.placeholders.text_width.desc` | Returns the width in pixels of the given text.&lt;br&gt;Useful for calculating element positions or sizes based on text width. |  |
| `fancymenu.placeholders.clipboard_content` | Clipboard Content |  |
| `fancymenu.placeholders.clipboard_content.desc` | Returns the current text content of the system's clipboard&lt;br&gt;or an empty text if nothing is currently stored in the clipboard. |  |
| `fancymenu.placeholders.player_team` | Scoreboard Player Team |  |
| `fancymenu.placeholders.player_team.desc` | Returns the current team of the given player&lt;br&gt;or an empty value if the player has no team. |  |
| `fancymenu.placeholders.categories.scoreboard` | Scoreboard |  |
| `fancymenu.placeholders.scoreboard.score` | Scoreboard Score |  |
| `fancymenu.placeholders.scoreboard.score.desc` | Returns the score value of a player for a specific objective.&lt;br&gt;Returns an empty value if the player has no score for that objective.&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;Required values:&lt;br&gt;- player: The player name to check&lt;br&gt;- objective: The objective name to get the score from |  |
| `fancymenu.placeholders.scoreboard.objectives_list` | Scoreboard Objectives List |  |
| `fancymenu.placeholders.scoreboard.objectives_list.desc` | Returns a list of all objectives in the scoreboard.&lt;br&gt;Each objective name is separated by the specified separator.&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;Optional values:&lt;br&gt;- separator: The text to separate objectives (default: ', ') |  |
| `fancymenu.placeholders.scoreboard.tracked_players` | Scoreboard Tracked Players |  |
| `fancymenu.placeholders.scoreboard.tracked_players.desc` | Returns a list of all players tracked by the scoreboard.&lt;br&gt;Players who have any score in any objective will be listed.&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;Optional values:&lt;br&gt;- separator: The text to separate player names (default: ', ') |  |
| `fancymenu.placeholders.scoreboard.display_slot` | Scoreboard Display Slot |  |
| `fancymenu.placeholders.scoreboard.display_slot.desc` | Returns the name of the objective displayed in a specific slot.&lt;br&gt;Returns an empty value if no objective is assigned to that slot.&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;Required values:&lt;br&gt;- slot: The display slot to check&lt;br&gt;  Valid slots: sidebar, list, below_name, or team color slots&lt;br&gt;  (e.g., sidebar.team.red, sidebar.team.blue) |  |
| `fancymenu.placeholders.scoreboard.has_score` | Scoreboard Has Score |  |
| `fancymenu.placeholders.scoreboard.has_score.desc` | Checks if a player has a score for a specific objective.&lt;br&gt;Returns 'true' if the player has a score, 'false' otherwise.&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;Required values:&lt;br&gt;- player: The player name to check&lt;br&gt;- objective: The objective name to check |  |
| `fancymenu.placeholders.scoreboard.objective_display_name` | Scoreboard Objective Display Name |  |
| `fancymenu.placeholders.scoreboard.objective_display_name.desc` | Returns the display name of an objective.&lt;br&gt;Can return as plain text or JSON format for advanced formatting.&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;Required values:&lt;br&gt;- objective: The objective name&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;Optional values:&lt;br&gt;- as_json: Set to 'true' to get JSON format (default: 'false') |  |
| `fancymenu.placeholders.scoreboard.objective_criteria` | Scoreboard Objective Criteria |  |
| `fancymenu.placeholders.scoreboard.objective_criteria.desc` | Returns the criteria type of an objective.&lt;br&gt;Common criteria: dummy, trigger, deathCount, playerKillCount,&lt;br&gt;totalKillCount, health, food, air, armor, xp, level, etc.&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;Required values:&lt;br&gt;- objective: The objective name to check |  |
| `fancymenu.placeholders.scoreboard.objective_render_type` | Scoreboard Objective Render Type |  |
| `fancymenu.placeholders.scoreboard.objective_render_type.desc` | Returns how an objective's scores are displayed.&lt;br&gt;Possible values: 'integer' (normal numbers) or 'hearts' (heart icons).&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;Required values:&lt;br&gt;- objective: The objective name to check |  |
| `fancymenu.placeholders.scoreboard.player_scores_list` | Scoreboard Player Scores List |  |
| `fancymenu.placeholders.scoreboard.player_scores_list.desc` | Returns a formatted list of all scores for a specific player.&lt;br&gt;Use %%objective%% and %%score%% in the format to customize output.&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;Required values:&lt;br&gt;- player: The player name to list scores for&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;Optional values:&lt;br&gt;- separator: Text between each score entry (default: ', ')&lt;br&gt;- format: How to format each entry (default: '%%objective%%: %%score%%') |  |
| `fancymenu.placeholders.scoreboard.objective_count` | Scoreboard Objective Count |  |
| `fancymenu.placeholders.scoreboard.objective_count.desc` | Returns the total number of objectives in the scoreboard.&lt;br&gt;Returns '0' if no objectives exist. |  |
| `fancymenu.placeholders.world.players_list` | World Players List |  |
| `fancymenu.placeholders.world.players_list.desc` | Returns a list of all players currently in the world.&lt;br&gt;Player names are sorted alphabetically.&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;Optional values:&lt;br&gt;- separator: The text to separate player names (default: ', ') |  |
| `fancymenu.placeholders.replace_text` | Replace Text |  |
| `fancymenu.placeholders.replace_text.desc` | Replaces text in a string using either literal text or regular expressions (regex).&lt;br&gt;Supports both single replacement and replace-all operations.&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;Required values:&lt;br&gt;- text: The input text to perform replacements on&lt;br&gt;- search: The text or regex pattern to search for&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;Optional values:&lt;br&gt;- replacement: The replacement text (default: empty string)&lt;br&gt;- use_regex: Set to 'true' to use regex patterns (default: 'false')&lt;br&gt;- replace_all: Set to 'true' to replace all occurrences, otherwise only first occurrence (default: 'true')&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;When using regex, you can use capture groups like $1, $2, etc. |  |
| `fancymenu.placeholders.nbt_data_get` | Get NBT Data |  |
| `fancymenu.placeholders.nbt_data_get.desc` | Gets NBT data from entities and blocks (similar to '/data get' command).&lt;br&gt;Returns the value at the specified NBT path.&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;§lTIP: §rUse the '/data get' command in-game without setting a path&lt;br&gt;to easily get all available NBT paths for an entity or block!&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;Required values:&lt;br&gt;- source_type: The data source type ('entity' or 'block')&lt;br&gt;- nbt_path: The NBT path to query (e.g. 'foodLevel', 'Pos[0]', 'Inventory[0].id')&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;Conditional values:&lt;br&gt;- entity_selector: Entity selector when source_type is 'entity'&lt;br&gt;  Supports: @s (self), @p (nearest player), @e (nearest entity),&lt;br&gt;  UUID, or entity name&lt;br&gt;- block_pos: Block position when source_type is 'block'&lt;br&gt;  Format: 'x y z' (e.g. '100 64 -200')&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;Optional values:&lt;br&gt;- scale: Scale factor for numeric values (default: 1.0)&lt;br&gt;- return_type: How to return the data (default: 'value')&lt;br&gt;  Options: 'value' (numeric/size), 'string' (text value),&lt;br&gt;  'snbt' (formatted NBT), 'json' (JSON format)&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;Returns empty string if data cannot be accessed or path is invalid. |  |
| `fancymenu.placeholders.player_has_tag` | Player Has Tag |  |
| `fancymenu.placeholders.player_has_tag.desc` | Checks if a specific player has a specific tag. Returns 'true' or 'false'.&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;These are the tags that you add via the '/tag' command. |  |
| `fancymenu.placeholders.player_tags_list` | Player Tags List |  |
| `fancymenu.placeholders.player_tags_list.desc` | Returns a list of all tags for a specific player, separated by the specified separator.&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;These are the tags that you add via the '/tag' command. |  |
| `fancymenu.placeholders.file_text` | File Text |  |
| `fancymenu.placeholders.file_text.desc` | Reads text from a local file or remote URL and returns it.&lt;br&gt;Supports reading entire content or only the last few lines.&lt;br&gt;Content is cached to improve performance:&lt;br&gt;- Local files: Re-read every 1 second&lt;br&gt;- URLs: Re-read every 10 seconds&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;Required values:&lt;br&gt;- path_or_url: The file path or URL to read from&lt;br&gt;  Local files: Use relative paths (e.g. 'config/myfile.txt')&lt;br&gt;  Remote files: Use HTTP/HTTPS URLs (e.g. 'https://example.com/data.txt')&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;Optional values:&lt;br&gt;- mode: How to read the content (default: 'all')&lt;br&gt;  Options: 'all' (entire content), 'last' (last X lines)&lt;br&gt;- separator: Text between lines when joining (default: '\n')&lt;br&gt;  Common options: '\n' (newline), ' \| ', ', ', ' '&lt;br&gt;- last_lines: Number of lines to return in 'last' mode (default: 1)&lt;br&gt;  Only used when mode is 'last'&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;Note: When mode is 'last' with last_lines set to 1,&lt;br&gt;the single line is returned without any separator.&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;Returns empty string if source is not cached yet, doesn't exist,&lt;br&gt;cannot be read, or if there's no internet connection for URLs. |  |
| `fancymenu.scroll_screen_normalizer` | Expose Scroll Area Content Of Current Screen: %s |  |
| `fancymenu.scroll_screen_normalizer.desc` | This will try to extract buttons and sliders from scroll areas&lt;br&gt;of the current screen, if it contains scroll areas.&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;This is useful to make, for example, options screens customizable. |  |
| `fancymenu.customization.items.text.markdown.tables` | Tables |  |
| `fancymenu.customization.items.text.markdown.tables.desc` | Customize the appearance of Markdown tables. |  |
| `fancymenu.customization.items.text.markdown.tables.show_header` | Show Header: %s |  |
| `fancymenu.customization.items.text.markdown.tables.alternate_row_colors` | Alternate Row Colors: %s |  |
| `fancymenu.customization.items.text.markdown.tables.line_color` | Table Line Color |  |
| `fancymenu.customization.items.text.markdown.tables.header_background_color` | Header Background Color |  |
| `fancymenu.customization.items.text.markdown.tables.row_background_color` | Row Background Color |  |
| `fancymenu.customization.items.text.markdown.tables.alternate_row_color` | Alternate Row Color |  |
| `fancymenu.customization.items.text.markdown.tables.alternate_row_color.desc` | The color used for every second row when 'Alternate Row Colors' is enabled. |  |
| `fancymenu.customization.items.text.markdown.tables.line_thickness` | Line Thickness |  |
| `fancymenu.customization.items.text.markdown.tables.cell_padding` | Cell Padding |  |
| `fancymenu.customization.items.text.markdown.tables.cell_padding.desc` | The padding inside table cells.&lt;br&gt;Higher values create more space between content and cell borders. |  |
| `fancymenu.customization.items.text.markdown.tables.margin` | Table Margin |  |
| `fancymenu.customization.items.text.markdown.tables.margin.desc` | The margin around tables.&lt;br&gt;Adds space above and below each table. |  |
| `fancymenu.placeholders.uppercase_text` | Uppercase Text |  |
| `fancymenu.placeholders.uppercase_text.desc` | Converts the input text to uppercase letters.&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;Example:&lt;br&gt;Input: Hello World&lt;br&gt;Output: HELLO WORLD |  |
| `fancymenu.placeholders.lowercase_text` | Lowercase Text |  |
| `fancymenu.placeholders.lowercase_text.desc` | Converts the input text to lowercase letters.&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;Example:&lt;br&gt;Input: HELLO WORLD&lt;br&gt;Output: hello world |  |
| `fancymenu.elements.checkbox` | Checkbox |  |
| `fancymenu.elements.checkbox.desc` | A clickable checkbox that can execute actions when toggled.&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;Use the '$$value' placeholder in its actions and loading requirements&lt;br&gt;to get the current checkbox state (true/false). |  |
| `fancymenu.elements.checkbox.textures` | Textures |  |
| `fancymenu.elements.checkbox.background_texture_normal` | Normal Background Texture |  |
| `fancymenu.elements.checkbox.background_texture_normal.desc` | The normal background texture. The default texture is 20x20 pixels. |  |
| `fancymenu.elements.checkbox.background_texture_hover` | Hover Background Texture |  |
| `fancymenu.elements.checkbox.background_texture_hover.desc` | The hover background texture. The default texture is 20x20 pixels. |  |
| `fancymenu.elements.checkbox.background_texture_inactive` | Inactive Background Texture |  |
| `fancymenu.elements.checkbox.background_texture_inactive.desc` | The inactive background texture. The default texture is 20x20 pixels. |  |
| `fancymenu.elements.checkbox.checkmark_texture` | Checkmark Texture |  |
| `fancymenu.elements.checkbox.checkmark_texture.desc` | The texture displayed when the checkbox is checked.&lt;br&gt;Leave empty to use the default checkmark.&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;The default checkmark texture is 20x20 pixels. |  |
| `fancymenu.editor.elements.checkbox.manage_actions.desc` | Actions will be executed when the checkbox is toggled.&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;You can use the '$$value' placeholder in actions&lt;br&gt;to get the current checkbox state (true/false). |  |
| `fancymenu.settings.caching.placeholders.set` | Set Placeholder Caching Duration [MS] |  |
| `fancymenu.settings.caching.placeholders.set.desc` | The amount of time in milliseconds FancyMenu should cache&lt;br&gt;replaced text of placeholders to make them less performance-heavy.&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;Setting this to 0 will disable caching.&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;§l§xCaching can cause unexpected behaviour in complex setups and action scripts! |  |
| `fancymenu.settings.caching.requirements.set` | Set Requirement Caching Duration [MS] |  |
| `fancymenu.settings.caching.requirements.set.desc` | The amount of time in milliseconds FancyMenu should cache&lt;br&gt;the state of requirements to make them less performance-heavy.&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;Setting this to 0 will disable caching.&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;§l§xCaching can cause unexpected behaviour in complex setups and action scripts! |  |
| `fancymenu.requirements.is_mcef_loaded` | Is MCEF Loaded |  |
| `fancymenu.requirements.is_mcef_loaded.desc` | Checks if the MCEF mod is loaded.&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;This mod is needed for the Browser element and for all&lt;br&gt;video-related features like the Video element and menu background. |  |
| `fancymenu.actions.send_http_request` | Send HTTP Request |  |
| `fancymenu.actions.send_http_request.desc` | Sends an HTTP request to a web target with configurable parameters.&lt;br&gt;&lt;br&gt;This action allows you to send data to REST APIs, webhooks, or any HTTP endpoint.&lt;br&gt;Supports various authentication methods, custom headers, and different request types. |  |
| `fancymenu.actions.send_http_request.edit_value` | Edit HTTP Request Configuration |  |
| `fancymenu.actions.send_http_request.edit.url` | Target URL |  |
| `fancymenu.actions.send_http_request.edit.method` | Request Method: %s |  |
| `fancymenu.actions.send_http_request.edit.method.title` | Request Method |  |
| `fancymenu.actions.send_http_request.edit.content_type` | Content Type |  |
| `fancymenu.actions.send_http_request.edit.body` | Request Body |  |
| `fancymenu.actions.send_http_request.edit.timeout` | Timeout (seconds) |  |
| `fancymenu.actions.send_http_request.edit.log_response` | Log Response: %s |  |
| `fancymenu.actions.send_http_request.edit.auth_type` | Authentication Type: %s |  |
| `fancymenu.actions.send_http_request.edit.auth_type.title` | Authentication Type |  |
| `fancymenu.actions.send_http_request.auth.none` | None |  |
| `fancymenu.actions.send_http_request.auth.basic` | Basic Authentication |  |
| `fancymenu.actions.send_http_request.auth.bearer` | Bearer Token |  |
| `fancymenu.actions.send_http_request.auth.api_key` | API Key |  |
| `fancymenu.actions.send_http_request.edit.auth_username` | Username |  |
| `fancymenu.actions.send_http_request.edit.auth_password` | Password |  |
| `fancymenu.actions.send_http_request.edit.auth_token` | Bearer Token |  |
| `fancymenu.actions.send_http_request.edit.auth_api_key_header` | API Key Header Name |  |
| `fancymenu.actions.send_http_request.edit.auth_api_key` | API Key Value |  |
| `fancymenu.actions.send_http_request.edit.headers` | Custom Headers |  |
| `fancymenu.actions.send_http_request.edit.add_header` | Add Header |  |
| `fancymenu.actions.send_http_request.edit.remove_header` | Remove |  |
| `fancymenu.actions.send_http_request.edit.header_key` | Header Key |  |
| `fancymenu.actions.send_http_request.edit.header_value` | Header Value |  |

### modernfix-forge-5.27.85+mc1.20.1.jar

| 键 | 英文原文 | 现有中文值 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `modernfix.redirector_installed` | ModernFix detected that Redirector is installed. Redirector is known to cause many strange and hard-to-debug issues, and removing it is strongly recommended. |  |
| `modernfix.option.mixin.perf.dynamic_resources.ldlib` | LDLib compatibility patch for dynamic resources |  |
| `modernfix.option.mixin.perf.compress_unihex_font` | Stores the glyphs for the Unicode font more efficiently. Kudos to @AnAwesomGuy for the trick. |  |
| `modernfix.option.mixin.bugfix.restore_old_dragon_movement` | Fixes MC-272431, which tracks the ender dragon being unable to dive to the portal as it did in 1.13 and older. This causes the dragon to fly quite a bit differently from what modern players are used to and also patches out one-cycling, so it's not enabled by default. Thanks to Jukitsu for identifying the regression in the vanilla code. |  |
| `modernfix.option.mixin.bugfix.missing_block_entities` | Hypixel sends chunks to the client that are missing some block entity data, which makes chests etc. appear invisible. This 'fixes' the problem by creating the needed data on the client. Has no effect for properly behaved servers or in singleplayer. |  |
| `modernfix.option.mixin.bugfix.buffer_builder_leak` | Attempts to work around mods constructing BufferBuilder objects that leak memory. This can cause the JVM to crash with an hs_err_pid file in some rare cases. If you experience this type of crash, try disabling this option. |  |
| `modernfix.option.mixin.bugfix.extra_experimental_screen` | Fixes the experimental feature warning being shown still being shown the first time you reopen a world that was created as experimental. |  |
| `modernfix.option.mixin.bugfix.forge_at_inject_error` | Fixes a major oversight in Forge's early error handling code that causes some parts of mods to load, but not others, which usually results in the game crashing rather than displaying the intended error screen. This issue was fixed in NeoForge, and eventually fixed in new enough versions of Forge. |  |
| `modernfix.option.mixin.bugfix.world_screen_skipped` | Fixes MC-251068, where deleting the last world takes you back to an empty world list. |  |
| `modernfix.option.mixin.feature.blockentity_incorrect_thread` | **This is a debug option and should not be enabled for regular gameplay.** Attempts to detect mods interacting with block entities on the wrong thread and crash the game with more information, rather than an unclear ConcurrentModificationException |  |
| `modernfix.option.mixin.feature.cause_lag_by_disabling_threads` | Disables the game's use of worker threads for the server and for chunk rendering. **This will make performance worse on almost all hardware**, and should only be enabled on a client, when you know exactly what you are doing, and probably only on hardware with very few (2 or less) physical CPU cores. In those rare cases, it can reduce lag spikes since there is less contention over the limited cores. |  |
| `modernfix.option.mixin.feature.registry_event_progress` | Runs the Forge loading screen on a background thread for part of the loading process, to allow progress to be shown while content is being registered. May have compatibility issues with some GPU drivers (e.g. macOS), so do not enable it by default in a modpack. |  |
| `modernfix.option.mixin.feature.remove_chat_signing` | Prevents the Minecraft client from obtaining the keypair for chat signing. This disables the client's ability to sign chat messages (like No Chat Reports, but in a simpler, albeit less user-friendly way). |  |
| `modernfix.option.mixin.feature.remove_telemetry` | Prevents the Minecraft client from sending telemetry to Mojang. The telemetry is generally not relevant to them anyway for a modded instance. |  |
| `modernfix.option.mixin.perf.chunk_meshing` | Implements some minor optimizations to the vanilla chunk meshing logic (they will have no effect with a mod that replaces chunk rendering entirely). |  |
| `modernfix.option.mixin.perf.faster_structure_location` | Improves the speed at which structures like buried treasure can be located. |  |
| `modernfix.option.mixin.perf.forge_registry_alloc` | Fixes more oversights in Forge that lead to excessive allocation in hot registry methods. |  |
| `modernfix.option.mixin.perf.memoize_creative_tab_build` | Improves on vanilla's existing caching for creative tab contents in a way that is compatible with the timing requirements of mods like JEI/EMI. This can reduce the lag spike when opening the creative inventory for the first time in a modpack. |  |
| `modernfix.option.mixin.perf.potential_spawns_alloc` | Optimizes the Forge event for finding potential mobs that can spawn. This reduces allocations and the overhead of rebuilding a weighted list when no mods modify the potential spawns. |  |
| `modernfix.option.mixin.perf.ticking_chunk_alloc` | Optimizes chunk ticking in vanilla to reduce allocations. |  |
| `modernfix.option.mixin.perf.worldgen_allocation` | Optimizes some world generation logic in vanilla to reduce object allocations. |  |
| `modernfix.option.mixin.feature.suppress_narrator_stacktrace` | Prevents the game from logging a very long stacktrace when the narrator fails to load on Linux (usually caused by not having libflite installed). |  |

| `gui.xaero_wm_detect_ambiguous_y` | Detect Ambiguous Y In Discovered |  |
| `gui.xaero_wm_box_detect_ambiguous_y` | A single pixel on the map can contain multiple transparent layers and an opaque block under them. In older non-cache map data files, only the Y level (height) of the opaque block is stored in such cases, which often makes it unsafe to blindly teleport to the stored Y coordinate. When this setting is ON, the mod treats such map pixels as it would undiscovered chunks. It defaults to 2D waypoints and disables direct teleportation to such pixels. Disable this at your own risk! |  |

| `gui.xaero_wm_box_adjust_height_for_carpetlike_blocks` | Subtract 1 from the height value for short carpetlike blocks, e.g. carpet, 1-layer snow, lilypad etc. This prevents such blocks from causing harsh shading on the map like a full block. Waypoints and teleportation over these blocks should make more sense too. |  |

| `gui.xaero_box_cave_mode_not_allowed` | Cave Mode (not allowed in settings) |  |
| `gui.xaero_wm_cave_mode_start_auto` | auto |  |
| `gui.xaero_wm_cave_mode_start` | Cave Mode Top Y |  |
| `gui.xaero_wm_cave_mode_type` | Cave Mode Type |  |
| `gui.xaero_wm_box_cave_mode_type` | The cave mode type to use when cave mode is enabled. &lt;br&gt; &lt;br&gt; OFF - display above ground even when cave mode is automatically or manually enabled (same as old world map versions without cave mode) &lt;br&gt; Layered - separate cave maps into multiple layers depending on the used top Y &lt;br&gt; Full - ignore the used top Y, use a single layer for cave maps and map everything from the world top to the world bottom |  |
| `gui.xaero_wm_default_cave_mode_type` | Default Cave Mode Type |  |
| `gui.xaero_wm_box_default_cave_mode_type` | The default cave mode type assigned to dimensions visited for the first time in a world/server. You can read more about cave mode types by clicking the cave mode icon on the map screen. |  |
| `gui.xaero_wm_cave_mode_type_layered` | Layered |  |
| `gui.xaero_wm_cave_mode_type_full` | Full |  |
| `gui.xaero_auto_cave_mode` | Auto Cave Mode |  |
| `gui.xaero_box_auto_cave_mode` | How the mod should determine the "auto" top Y when viewing the full screen map. The ceiling size refers to the size of a square ceiling of solid blocks that has to be detected above you to activate the cave mode. The solid blocks don't have to be on the same Y level. &lt;br&gt; &lt;br&gt; Minimap - if installed, match the minimap mod's cave mode, otherwise use the ceiling size 3x3 |  |
| `gui.xaero_auto_cave_mode_minimap` | Minimap |  |

| `gui.xaero_wm_cave_mode_toggle_timer` | Cave Mode Toggle Timer |  |
| `gui.xaero_wm_box_cave_mode_toggle_timer` | The minimum time to wait between toggles of cave mode to prevent flickering when quickly switching between being under blocks and not. This only applies when "Auto Cave Mode" isn't redirecting to the minimap mod. The latest minimap mod has a separate setting for this as well. |  |
| `effect.xaeroworldmap.no_cave_maps` | No WM Cave Maps |  |
| `effect.xaeroworldmap.no_cave_maps_harmful` | No WM Cave Maps |  |
| `gui.xaero_wm_biome_blending` | Biome Blending |  |
| `gui.xaero_wm_box_biome_blending` | Smooth out biome color edges by sampling biome colors for multiple blocks for every block on the map. Turning this off can significantly improve performance when biome color calculation is modded to be much more heavyweight than vanilla. |  |

| `gui.xaero_export_screen_exporting` | Exporting... Will temporarily appear frozen. Might take a while! |  |
| `gui.xaero_export_option_full` | Force Full Map |  |
| `gui.xaero_box_export_option_full` | Export the whole map even if you've made a map selection. |  |
| `gui.xaero_export_option_multiple_images` | Multiple Unscaled Images |  |
| `gui.xaero_box_export_option_multiple_images` | Export the map as multiple unscaled images no matter how big it is. This does not have the same memory limitations as a single image. |  |
| `gui.xaero_export_option_nighttime` | Nighttime |  |
| `gui.xaero_box_export_option_nighttime` | Export the map with nighttime lighting. This doesn't affect cave mode maps. |  |
| `gui.xaero_export_option_scale_down_square` | Max Single Image Size |  |
| `gui.xaero_export_option_scale_down_square_value` | %1$dx%1$d reg |  |
| `gui.xaero_export_option_scale_down_square_unscaled` | Unscaled |  |
| `gui.xaero_box_export_option_scale_down_square` | The size, in regions, of a square image equivalent to the resolution that a large exported map will be scaled down to if necessary. For example, 20x20 stands for 400 regions, 512x512 blocks/pixels each. The resulting image doesn't have to be a square. 10x40 is also 400 regions. &lt;br&gt; &lt;br&gt; Your computer or the Java heap size might not be able to handle higher export resolutions. |  |
| `gui.xaero_export_option_highlights` | Include Active Highlights |  |
| `gui.xaero_box_export_option_highlights` | Export the map with all currently enabled highlights (e.g. claims) applied to the map. When exporting the full map, highlights very far from your discovered map won't be included. |  |
| `gui.xaero_world_save` | World Save |  |
| `gui.xaero_map_writing_distance` | Map Writing Distance |  |
| `gui.xaero_box_map_writing_distance` | The maximum X and Z distance in chunks at which chunks can be loaded or updated on a non-world-save map. For the "Unlimited" option, go all the way to the left. World save maps always generate within your render distance |  |
| `gui.xaero_map_writing_distance_unlimited` | Unlimited |  |
| `gui.xaero_full_reload` | Reload All Regions |  |
| `gui.xaero_box_full_reload` | Reload all regions on the map without having to manually view them. The reloading process will work in the background and turn off when it's done. It is important to note that going to another dimension will pause it until you come back and changing maps will fully cancel it. |  |
| `gui.xaero_full_resave` | Convert All Regions |  |
| `gui.xaero_box_full_resave` | Reload and resave all regions on the map without having to manually visit them. World save maps will be converted into normal map data saved to a separate map instance "Converted World Save", stored in the folder "cm$converted", which can be transferred to multiplayer map instances for the same world, using file manager. The reloading process will work in the background and turn off when it's done. It is important to note that going to another dimension will pause it until you come back and changing maps will fully cancel it. |  |
| `gui.xaero_full_reload_in_progress` | Full map reload is in progress... |  |
| `gui.xaero_reload_viewed_regions` | Reload Viewed Regions |  |
| `gui.xaero_box_reload_viewed_regions` | Reload every region that is displayed on your screen once. |  |
| `gui.xaero_converted_world_save` | Converted World Save |  |
| `gui.xaero_unknown_dimension_type1` | Currently unknown dimension type! |  |
| `gui.xaero_unknown_dimension_type2` | The map functions are limited. Visiting the dimension once might help. |  |

| `gui.xaero_wm_dimension_teleport_command` | Map Cross-Dimension Teleport Command |  |
| `gui.xaero_wm_teleport_not_connected` | You are trying to teleport to a map that is not connected to the current confirmed/auto one. If you are sure that this map is from your current sub-server/world save, then you can enable teleportation by adding a connection in the map selection screen -&gt; Connect. But beware, if you connect unrelated maps and teleport, then there is a good chance you will suffocate in a block or die from fall damage, so be careful and connect only the right ones. |  |
| `gui.xaero_wm_teleport_never_confirmed` | You cannot teleport yet because you have not yet confirmed a single map in your current dimension. Please confirm a map in your current dimension or change map selection type to an automatic one. |  |

| `gui.xaero_disconnect_map` | Disconnect |  |

| `gui.xaero_wm_box_cave_mode_allowed` | Cave mode on the full screen world map. It's a mapping mode that can go deeper than the surface blocks, mainly to display underground caves and interiors of buildings. Cave mode is usually enabled automatically (see "Auto Cave Mode"), but can also be enabled manually on the world map screen. |  |
| `gui.xaero_wm_cave_mode_allowed_dimensions` | Full Screen Cave Mode Dimensions |  |
| `gui.xaero_wm_box_cave_mode_allowed_dimensions` | A list of dimension IDs, separated by comas, where cave mode should work on the full screen world map. If the list is empty (just []), cave mode works in every dimension. |  |
| `gui.xaero_world_map_redirect_legacy` | Disabled by a server plugin! |  |
| `gui.xaero_world_map_config_redirect_fairplay` | Disabled by fair-play mode enforced by the server! |  |
| `gui.xaero_world_map_config_redirect_effect` | Disabled by a status effect! |  |
| `gui.xaero_wm_unit_s` | s |  |
| `gui.xaero_wm_unit_ms` | ms |  |
| `gui.xaero_wm_default_teleport_command` | Default Map Teleport Command |  |
| `gui.xaero_wm_default_teleport_command_dimension` | Default Cross-Dim Teleport Command |  |
| `gui.xaero_wm_default_player_teleport_command` | Default Player Teleport Command |  |
| `gui.xaero_wm_use_default` | Use Default |  |
| `gui.xaero_wm_box_map_teleport_command` | The chat commands used on this world/server to teleport you to positions on the map. Stored per world/server, not part of the config profile. |  |
| `gui.xaero_map_item` | Fullscreen Map Item |  |
| `gui.xaero_box_map_item` | The ID of an item required in the hotbar for the fullscreen world map to function. Set it to "-" to have no item requirement. |  |
| `gui.xaero_wm_display_minimap_radar` | Minimap Radar |  |
| `gui.xaero_wm_box_display_minimap_radar` | Display minimap radar on the fullscreen map. Only works when the Xaero's Minimap mod is installed. |  |
| `gui.xaero_render_worldmap_waypoints` | Render World Map Waypoints |  |
| `gui.xaero_wm_display_tracked_players` | Display Tracked Players |  |
| `gui.xaero_wm_reset_config_profile_default` | Reset Profile Defaults |  |
| `gui.xaero_wm_reset_config_profile_default_message` | Are you sure you want to restore the default values in the config profile? |  |
| `gui.xaero_wm_reset_config_profile_default_message2` | This action cannot be undone! Non-profile options aren't affected. |  |
| `gui.xaero_wm_max_loaded_regions` | Max Loaded Regions |  |
| `gui.xaero_wm_box_max_loaded_regions` | The approximate maximum number of 512x512 map regions to keep loaded, including "branch" regions rendered at lower (further) zoom levels. Other factors can increase/decrease the actual effective limit. A higher number improves performance but uses more RAM and VRAM which you can run out of. |  |
| `gui.xaero_wm_unlimited_zoom_in` | Unlimited Fullscreen Zoom-in |  |
| `gui.xaero_wm_box_unlimited_zoom_in` | Not actually unlimited but allows you to zoom in the fullscreen map way beyond the usual limit. |  |
| `gui.xaero_wm_unlimited_zoom_out` | Unlimited Fullscreen Zoom-out |  |
| `gui.xaero_wm_box_unlimited_zoom_out` | Experimental! &lt;br&gt; Not actually unlimited but allows you to zoom out the fullscreen map way beyond the usual limit. It is not properly optimized for regular gameplay and should only be used when you don't care about that. |  |
| `gui.xaero_box_rendering_waypoints_server_enforced` | The world map waypoint rendering setting is enforced by the server! |  |
| `gui.xaero_wm_skip_world_render` | Skip World Render |  |
| `gui.xaero_wm_box_skip_world_render` | Skip rendering the world in the background while the world map screen is open which significantly increases the framerate. However, when this is enabled, the framerate is also manually limited to around 625 to avoid issues that come from very high framerates (such as with video recording or streaming). Some mods might not be compatible with this feature and turning it off should fix any issues but with the drawback of (usually) worse framerates. |  |
| `gui.xaero_wm_ignore_map_file_locks` | Ignore Map File Locks |  |
| `gui.xaero_wm_box_ignore_map_file_locks` | Proceed with using a map instance even if it is already being used by another process. This will cause region loading failures. Do not use this unless you know what you're doing. Relog after turning this on. |  |

| `gui.xaero_hop_error_too_many` | Too many arguments! |  |
| `gui.xaero_hop_error_source` | Hop To Coordinates |  |
| `gui.xaero_attached_camera_button_enabled` | Camera is attached to your character |  |
| `gui.xaero_attached_camera_button_disabled` | Camera is not attached to your character |  |
| `gui.xaero_wm_display_server_chunk_radius` | Display Server Chunk Radius |  |
| `gui.xaero_wm_box_display_server_chunk_radius` | Display a square overlay on the world map around your character corresponding to the server chunk radius (server render distance). |  |
| `gui.xaero_wm_force_fast_map_writing` | Force Fast Writing |  |
| `gui.xaero_wm_box_force_fast_map_writing` | Bad for performance but good for mapping out servers at very high movement speeds. |  |
| `gui.xaero_map_distances` | Display Map Distances |  |
| `gui.xaero_box_map_distances` | Display distances to the positions or elements on the map that you right click. For positions, the distance displayed is 2-dimensional. For elements, such as waypoints and tracked players, it is 3-dimensional where applicable. |  |

Insert: Insert items into the bound block; 2. Split: Input items into the bound block without stacking across different slots (e.g., for Cook Pot); 3. Extract: Extract target items from the bound block; 4. Throw: Throw items as entities at the bound position; 5. PickUp: Pick up target item entities from the bound position. |  |
| 1.20.1-maid_storage_manager-1.15.6.jar | `assets/touhou_little_maid/patchouli_books/memorizable_gensokyo/en_us/entries/maid_storage_manager/9_craft_guide_settings.json::/pages/7/text` | 6. Use: Right-click bound position with input item to get the output item (both can be empty, e.g., for log stripping); 7. Destroy: Destroy bound block with input item and get the output item; 8. Idle: Wait at bound position for specified time (press T/S to switch time units, scroll to adjust – e.g., to avoid processing timeouts). |  |
| 1.20.1-maid_storage_manager-1.15.6.jar | `assets/touhou_little_maid/patchouli_books/memorizable_gensokyo/en_us/entries/maid_storage_manager/9_craft_guide_settings.json::/pages/9/text` | Sometimes a part of a recipe is unpredictable (probable output, side-products) and the maid will wait without the specified output until the recipe times out. In cases of uncertainty such as these, the step can be made ‘optional’ and the maid will perform the corresponding action and move on to the next crafting step, regardless of whether it is successful or not. |  |
